Conclusion: 该工作为1C代码检索提供了首个开放基准和专用模型，证明了合成数据增强与MRL在低资源领域代码检索中的有效性，同时通过重叠审计验证了结果的可靠性。256维截断的实用意义表明模型在保持检索质量的同时可显著降低部署成本。

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>

*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代式代理修正框架，用于不完整多模态情感分析，通过门控残差修正逐步细化从非语言模态构建的语言代理，并依据语言可靠性评分自适应融合，在多种缺失模态设置下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态输入常常不完整或被破坏，削弱跨模态互补性并引入误导信息。现有基于代理的方法依赖一次性代理构建来补偿退化的语言信息，但初始代理可能粗糙不可靠，过早注入会传播初始错误并损害情感预测。

Method: 提出迭代代理修正框架：(1) 从非语言模态构建语言导向代理；(2) 在多模态情境下通过门控残差修正逐步细化代理；(3) 根据估计的语言可靠性评分，将修正后的代理与观测语言表示自适应融合；(4) 引入分阶段潜在修正目标，以完整语言表示作为训练时语义锚点来稳定代理细化轨迹。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS数据集上的多种缺失模态设置下，该框架一致优于竞争基线，在不完整输入下实现稳健的情感预测。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>

TL;DR: HealMed是一个由23位医师和医学专家历时两年、覆盖九国九语种构建的医学多语言评测基准，包含1000条示例、三种任务格式（MCQA、NLI、开放式问答），每个翻译都经两位专家复审。评测发现：低资源语言性能下降最明显但幅度因语言和模型而异；最强闭源模型跨语言最稳定，而开源和医学专用模型差距更大且不稳定；医学专业化并不能保证多语言鲁棒性；专家修订翻译可提升或降低性能，说明翻译质量显著影响跨语言评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建HealMed基准：汇集9个数据集、覆盖9种语言、每种语言1000个示例，包含MCQA、NLI和开放式问答三种任务格式；由9个国家的23名医学专家历时两年开发；每项翻译均由两位精通英语和对应目标语的专家评估和修订。

Result: 发现性能在低资源语言中下降最明显，但差距大小因语言和模型而异；最强闭源模型跨语言最稳定，而开源及医学专用模型表现出更大且不一致的差距；医学专业化并不保证多语言鲁棒性；专家修订可能提升或降低性能，表明翻译质量对跨语言评测结果有显著影响。

Conclusion: 多语言医学评测需要高质量的专家审核翻译基准，翻译质量本身会显著影响模型性能评估结果，模型开发者在追求医学能力时应同时关注多语言鲁棒性，而非仅依赖医学领域专业化。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多语言水印评估框架，包含四个组件：经验校准的检测阈值、与阈值无关的伴随测量、三种质量测量范式、以及基于类型学族划分的广义熵分解。应用于六种水印方案、三种生成器、十一种语言，揭示了单语言单范式评估无法发现的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型水印方案几乎只在英语上评估，使用各方案自带的检测阈值和窄范围的质量测量。多语言部署暴露了在英语上无关紧要但在跨语言场景下决定结论的评估设计选择，亟需一个系统的多语言评估框架。

Method: 提出四组件评估框架：(1) 按部署环境经验校准检测阈值；(2) 与阈值无关的伴随测量以区分校准失败与检测失败；(3) 三种互斥的质量测量范式（分布性、配对语义、参考困惑度）；(4) 基于类型学语系划分的广义熵分解，量化跨语言差异来源。

Result: 在六种水印方案、三种开放权重生成器、十一语言（四种文字、八种类型学语系）、基础和指令微调两种模式下，框架揭示了单语言单范式评估无法揭示的失败模式。检测和质量层面的差异主要在类型学语系之间而非语系内部，表明水印的跨语言公平性差距是语言属性的结构性问题。

Conclusion: 跨语言公平性差距在水印方案中是结构性的，源于语言属性本身而非特定语言的独特性。现有单语言评估范式不足以指导多语言水印部署，需要采用本框架这种多层面、阈值校准且类型学感知的评估方法。

Abstract: Watermarking schemes for large language model output are evaluated almost exclusively on English text using each scheme's detection threshold and a narrow set of quality measurements. Multilingual deployment exposes evaluation-design choices that are inconsequential on English but determine conclusions cross-lingually. We propose an evaluation framework with four components: detection thresholds calibrated empirically per deployment context, a threshold-independent companion measurement that distinguishes calibration failures from detection failures, three disjoint quality measurement paradigms (distributional, paired-semantic, and reference-perplexity), and a generalized-entropy decomposition of cross-language disparity over a typological family partition. Applied to six watermarking schemes, three open-weight generators, eleven languages spanning four scripts and eight typological families, and both base and instruction-tuned regimes, the framework reveals failure modes that single-language single-paradigm evaluation cannot surface. Across detection and quality, observed disparity is predominantly between-family on the typological partition, indicating that cross-lingual fairness gaps in watermarking are structural to language properties rather than idiosyncratic to particular languages.

</details>

*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SABET-QA是一个针对时间知识图谱问答（TKGQA）的框架，通过迭代式多跳推理、双向实体-时间打分机制和槽位感知的上下文模块来对齐问题语义与时间知识图谱嵌入，在多个基准数据集上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的TKGQA方法依赖单遍推理管线，难以处理需要跨多个时间事实进行推理的多步查询，缺乏迭代细化推理状态的能力。

Method: 提出SABET-QA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 双向实体-时间打分机制，在多跳中迭代细化推理状态；(2) 槽位感知上下文模块，对齐问题语义与时间知识图谱嵌入；(3) 可微分工作记忆机制，支持渐进式假设精化；当有可用标签时，利用辅助时间边界提供粗粒度监督。

Result: 在CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ和TimeQuestions四个数据集上的实验表明，相比强基线方法取得一致改进，尤其在复杂的多步时间查询任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: SABET-QA通过迭代多跳推理和语义对齐机制，有效解决了嵌入方法在TKGQA中的单遍推理局限，为复杂时间推理任务提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [42] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OenoBench，一个包含3,266道选择题的葡萄酒领域知识基准，涵盖六大支柱和四个难度层级。该基准基于35个来源可验证的爬虫从政府注册表、同行评审期刊和Wikipedia/Wikidata提取的38,104个原子事实构建。论文的核心方法论贡献是LLM驱动管线：语言模型仅用于格式化事实和审核结果，但从不作为事实来源——每个声明都可追溯到URL。评估16种前沿模型配置发现：准确率范围53%-84%（o3以83.6%领先），推理模式提升仅在DeepSeek R1中显著，Anthropic存在+9pp自我偏好，开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率帕累托前沿，所有配置在闭卷可解项目上均遇到参数记忆上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识基准（如MMLU、GPQA）存在数据污染、来源不透明和事实无法验证等问题。此外，葡萄酒领域缺乏系统化、来源可追溯的知识评估基准。作者希望构建一个严格锚定于可验证来源的领域知识基准，确保每一道题目都能追溯到具体来源，同时利用LLM辅助构建但避免LLM引入幻觉，从而提供可靠的知识评估工具。

Method: 构建流程：(1)开发35个来源验证的爬虫，从INAO、TTB、OIV等政府注册表、同行评审期刊及Wikipedia/Wikidata提取38,104个原子事实；(2)LLM仅负责将已验证事实重新格式化并审核结果，绝不作为事实来源——每个声明都锚定到URL；(3)使用五种生成策略、五种生成器家族生成3,266道多选题；(4)九智能体审核系统配合人类黄金标准（Cohen's κ校准）进行评分；(5)构建涵盖区域、葡萄品种、葡萄栽培、酿酒、生产商、商业六大支柱和四个难度层级的体系。

Result: 评估16种前沿模型配置发现：(i)整体准确率53%-84%，o3以83.6%领先；(ii)推理模式提升集中在DeepSeek R1达到+6.8pp，但Claude Opus和Gemini Pro无此提升；(iii)Anthropic对其自身生成的问题有+9pp自我偏好，而Google呈-8pp反向偏好；(iv)前沿开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率帕累托前沿；(v)所有配置在闭卷可解项目上均提升约33pp，揭示参数记忆上限，只有上下文切片能避免。

Conclusion: OenoBench的贡献在于：(1)构建了首个来源可完全追溯的葡萄酒领域知识基准；(2)证明了"LLM辅助但非LLM作真"的管线能有效生成高质量评测数据；(3)揭示了大模型在领域知识上的自我偏好偏差和参数记忆局限。研究者以CC-BY-SA-4.0协议发布了语料、审核发现、人工审核应用和构建代码，为后续知识评估研究提供了透明的、可复现的基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>


### [43] [When Text and Numbers Disagree: Evidence Arbitration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20116)

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在面对文本摘要、数值观测和外部工具输出等相互冲突的证据源时如何进行仲裁决策。作者构建了一个受控合成基准，其中潜在风险轨迹同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，从而构造恰好只有一个证据源与真实标签一致的冲突场景。研究发现LLM的仲裁行为是系统性的：模型表现出文本与数字的偏好差异，对时间新近性的跟随比对显式可靠性线索更一致，且可能过度依赖外部预测即使它与直接上下文证据冲突。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于整合来自不同来源（文本摘要、数值观测、外部工具输出）的信息，但这些来源在支持相反决策时可能提供冲突证据。理解LLM如何仲裁这些冲突，对于评估工具增强决策系统的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 构建一个受控合成基准，通过潜在风险轨迹同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，设计恰好一个证据源与真实标签一致的冲突场景。独立操纵四个维度：模态（文本vs数字）、时间新近性、来源可靠性、证据来源（provenance）。在多个开源指令微调模型上进行测试。

Result: LLM的仲裁行为是系统性的而非随机的：模型表现出明显的文本与数字偏好差异；模型对时间新近性的跟随比对显式可靠性线索更一致；模型可能过度依赖外部预测，即使这些预测与直接上下文证据相冲突。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在整合异构证据时往往依赖启发式仲裁策略，而非深思熟虑的证据权衡，这暴露了工具增强决策系统中的一个失败模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where textual summaries, numerical observations, and external tool outputs may provide conflicting evidence. We study how LLMs arbitrate between such sources when they support opposing decisions. To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个专家验证的基准测试，用于评估LLM在前沿理论计算机科学（TCS）端到端研究中的表现。该基准包含175个来自2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA和COLT会议论文的实例，并带有专家验证的Lean形式化证明。评估发现当前模型无法可靠完成完整研究流程，其中自动形式化是最关键的瓶颈，同时研究品味（research taste）不足是另一个主要障碍。

Result: 最佳模型在自然语言声明到形式化定理陈述的翻译上仅达到11.5分，而在给出人类提供的形式化陈述时证明准确率可达28.6（Pass@8）；在自动化研究框架生成的64个声明中，仅6个通过专家评估和证明验证。

Conclusion: 当前LLM距离可靠完成完整TCS研究流程仍有很大差距，自动形式化是最突出的瓶颈，而有限的研究品味（即判断什么值得研究的能力）是通向自主TCS研究的另一大障碍。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [45] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Task-CoEvolve方法，通过自适应验证任务选择来优化LLM智能体harness。该方法与harness共同演化验证任务，利用方差加权采样聚焦于智能体能力边界附近的不确定性任务，并从部分评估中估计全量性能，在保持最终性能的同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体harness优化方法在每一轮迭代中都对固定的验证集进行完整评估，即使某些任务随着harness的演化变得不再具有区分度，仍然会产生高昂的评估成本。作者希望解决这一效率瓶颈，只选择具有信息量的任务进行评估。

Method: Task-CoEvolve基于一个关键观察：候选harness在某一任务上产生分歧时，该任务比那些一致解决或一致失败的任务更具区分度。方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于历史结果的方差加权采样，将评估集中在智能体能力边界附近的任务上，采样分布随harness演化自适应调整；(2) 通过考虑采样概率来估计全量分数，使得在不同迭代中评估不同子集时仍能进行一致的比较。

Result: 在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1上的实验表明，Task-CoEvolve一致优于固定子集基线，在匹配全量搜索的最终性能的同时，将优化过程中的评估数量减少了80%。

Conclusion: Task-CoEvolve证明了一种自适应验证任务选择策略可以在不牺牲最终性能的前提下大幅降低harness优化的评估成本，为大规模LLM智能体优化提供了更高效的路径。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [46] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IAR（注入-对齐-恢复）三阶段后训练框架，用于将固定文档语料库转化为参数化知识，实现无需检索的文档问答。该方法在多个模型家族和数据集上优于传统SFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理时无法检索源文档的情况下，往往难以回答关于有界文档集合的问题。现有方法（如持续预训练）在文档知识内化方面效果有限，需要一种更有效的方法将固定语料库转化为可用的参数化知识。

Method: 提出IAR三阶段后训练框架：1）Inject阶段将源文档转换为续写、改写和指令条件重建目标；2）Align阶段使用仅答案的QA监督调整模型；3）Recover阶段将领域适应模型与基础指令模型合并，以恢复通用能力。

Result: 在Common Corpus和CCI数据集上，跨Llama、Phi、Qwen和SmolLM模型家族，IAR在7/8的数据集-模型设置中在所有四项指标上优于Vanilla SFT，领域QA准确率平均提升3.6个百分点，通用性能（IFEval、MMLU和MSBench均值）平均提升12.1个百分点。扩展CC基线显示LoRA和FAPM可在个别通用指标上获胜，但IAR在达到领先或接近领先领域内化效果的方法中保持最强的通用能力。

Conclusion: IAR通过分离结构化文档知识注入、QA行为对齐和通用能力恢复三个步骤，有效提升了检索无关文档内化的领域-通用性能前沿，证明了该方法在多个模型和数据集上的普适性和优越性。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [47] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（TMI）方法，从自然计算机使用轨迹中自动发现潜在任务并归纳结构化任务模型，在受控实验中任务分组一致性达0.974，重构74.9%的执行步骤，派生技能使任务准确率提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然计算机使用轨迹（截图、鼠标键盘操作）是构建可审计、可复用工作任务模型的重要资源，但现有方法假设任务已知或单一工作流，只能产生步骤级摘要，无法处理真实工作中多线程交织的目标活动，需要一种能从无约束轨迹中发现潜在任务并归纳结构化模型的方法。

Method: TMI方法包含两个核心模块：(i)潜在任务发现——从无约束轨迹中解耦并发活动，发现隐藏任务；(ii)对每个潜在任务归纳任务模型，该模型配对层次目标模型（递归目标分解）与程序模型（组织执行的控制流）。

Result: 在受控的人类和智能体轨迹上，TMI恢复交织任务时与真实分组的一致性达0.974，重构74.9%的观察执行步骤，远超最强工作流归纳基线；从TMI任务模型派生的技能使保留任务的准确率比最强基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: TMI在自然计算机使用轨迹的任务发现和结构化任务模型归纳方面显著优于现有基线，其派生的技能模型具有实际应用价值，为计算机使用智能体从真实工作学习中提供了更有效的路径。

Abstract: Naturalistic computer-use traces, passively recorded screenshots and mouse or keyboard actions, are a valuable resource for deriving symbolic, auditable, and reusable models of how everyday work is done. Such models matter as computer-use agents enter real work, where agents need to learn how tasks are actually performed, and organizations need to audit and reuse that knowledge. However, inducing such task models is challenging, as activity is observed only as low-level events and real-world work is multi-threaded with interleaved goals. Existing methods assume a given task or a single workflow, and produce step-level summaries rather than structured task models. We introduce Task Model Induction (TMI), which (i) discovers the latent tasks in an unconstrained trace, disentangling concurrent activity, and (ii) for each latent task, induces a task model pairing a hierarchical objective model of recursive goal decomposition with a procedure model of the control flow that organized the execution. Intrinsically, on controlled human and agent trajectories, TMI recovers interleaved tasks with 0.974 agreement against ground-truth groupings and reconstructs 74.9% of the observed execution steps, far more than the strongest workflow induction baseline. Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [48] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出患者导向的医学报告解读（PMRI）任务及G-CARL框架，通过基于检索的原子声明验证和实例级加权检查清单，在强化学习框架下同时优化医学事实性和患者可理解性，并构建了MMedReport基准及临床医生设计的三维评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告的个性化解读需求日益增长，这既要求基于证据的医学事实性，又要求根据患者情境进行有效沟通。然而现有医学视觉-语言任务未能同时捕捉这两个关键需求，且这两个目标在可验证性上存在根本差异却紧密耦合，在传统监督微调和整体强化学习范式下难以联合优化。

Method: 提出G-CARL框架，一种基于证据的、对齐检查清单的强化学习方法。具体包括：（1）多源检索用于原子声明验证（保证事实性）；（2）上下文感知的实例级加权检查清单用于响应覆盖（兼顾用户需求满足和表达质量）；（3）在强化学习框架中提供结构化监督，同时不限制响应多样性。

Result: 构建了MMedReport真实世界基准和临床医生设计的三维评估协议。大量实验表明G-CARL在整体质量、声明级精确率和检查清单召回率上一致优于现有后训练基线方法；临床医生的成对偏好评估进一步确认G-CARL生成的解读更准确且更符合患者需求。

Conclusion: G-CARL通过将事实性验证与用户需求覆盖解耦为结构化监督信号，在强化学习框架下有效地同时优化了医学报告解读的准确性和可及性，为面向患者的医学多模态生成任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [49] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"双重用途概念"（dual-use concepts）这一新视角，并构建了ConceptGuard基准来评估大语言模型在概念层面上的遗忘（unlearning）能力。基准中的遗忘集和保留集在概念使用上显式互补，评估是意图敏感的，旨在最大化上下文分离以促进更安全的行为。研究发现现有遗忘技术在概念层面表现不佳，存在弱上下文分离、强遗忘-效用权衡、概念级控制一致性差等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法和基准仅基于不相交的遗忘/保留集和简单的事实召回来评估，无法捕捉遗忘的核心要求——在消除有害行为的同时保留良性和有益知识。有效遗忘必须在概念层面运作，而当前的评估框架无法体现这一点。

Method: 引入双重用途概念（既可有害又可良性使用的概念），构建ConceptGuard基准，其中遗忘集和保留集在概念使用上显式互补。评估是意图敏感的，目标是通过最大化上下文分离来促进更安全的行为，并使用ROUGE和概念级指标进行评估。

Result: 现有遗忘技术在ConceptGuard设置下表现不佳：显示弱上下文分离、ROUGE和概念级指标表现差，存在强烈的遗忘-效用权衡，上下文敏感性提升有限，且不同方法在概念级控制上的一致性差。

Conclusion: 当前遗忘技术无法在概念层面实现完整且安全的遗忘，需要开发与真实世界安全需求更契合的遗忘方法。该基准为未来研究提供了改进方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>

### [50] [Mapping General-Purpose AI Governance in Twenty AI Middle-Power Jurisdictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19278)
*Josephine Schwab,Nathan Naidoo,Ferruccio Barazzutti,Sheryn Lee,Caio Vieira Machado*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究系统梳理了20个AI中等强国（包括欧盟）针对通用AI（GPAI）的监管条款，将其映射到四个治理领域（系统性风险评估、评估与验证、带监控的禁令、严重事件报告），发现各国在监管形式上趋同但力度上分化：16个国家涉及至少三个领域，但只有约五分之一的条款属于具有约束力的法律，且大多数约束性工具未定义GPAI。制度基础设施同样呈现"继承式"特征——多数监管机构成立于GPAI之前，义务主要附着在应用层而非模型层，几乎没有评估机构拥有基于其发现采取行动的权力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最强大的通用AI模型主要在美国和中国构建，但其风险却是全球性的。既无前沿开发者又经济发达的区域性中等强国正在自行制定GPAI治理规则，然而缺乏对这些规则的系统性、条款级比较研究，不清楚各国在多大程度上真正覆盖了模型层的问责链条。

Method: 对包括欧盟在内的20个司法管辖区进行条款级映射分析，将GPAI相关条款归入四个治理领域（系统性风险评估、评估与验证、带监控和检测的禁令、严重事件报告），将"确认的缺失"也作为数据记录，同时分析监管机构的制度属性和法律约束力。

Result: 研究发现各国监管"形式趋同、力度分化"：16个司法管辖区涉及至少三个治理领域，但仅约五分之一条款在具有约束力的法律中，四分之三的约束性工具未定义GPAI；五分之四的监管机构职责早于GPAI存在；义务主要附着于应用层而非模型层；几乎每个评估机构都无权基于发现采取行动；名义上11个司法管辖区覆盖完整问责链，但仅5个在每个领域有超过一条条款，且欧盟之外没有司法管辖区对模型开发者施加有约束力的评估义务。

Conclusion: AI中等强国的GPAI监管呈现"继承式扩展"的特征：它们更多是在既有制度框架上延伸，而非全新设计针对模型层的治理。该数据集为研究人员和政策制定者提供了条款级基础，以识别哪些制度可以协调一致、哪些协调需要从零开始。

Abstract: The most capable general-purpose AI (GPAI) models are mostly built in two jurisdictions, the United States and China, but the risks they carry land globally. Regionally advanced economies hosting no frontier developer, which we call AI middle-powers, are writing their own rules to govern GPAI. This paper investigates which GPAI-relevant provisions these AI middle-powers have enacted, mapping twenty jurisdictions including the European Union at the level of the individual provision, across four governance areas that trace the accountability chain for the model layer: systemic risk assessment, evaluation and verification, prohibitions with monitoring and detection, and serious incident reporting. Confirmed absence is recorded as data alongside positive provision. We find that jurisdictions converge on form, but diverge on force. Sixteen engage in at least three of the four governance areas, yet only about one in five provisions sit in binding law, and three-quarters of the instruments that do bind do so without defining GPAI. The institutional infrastructure shows the same shape: four in five of the mapped governance actors hold mandates that predate GPAI, and obligations attach wherever the inherited regime already reached, which is the application layer rather than the model. Where these states engage the model layer, they build capacity to observe it rather than impose duties on those who build it, and almost every evaluation body was constituted without the power to act on what it finds. Nominal coverage of the full accountability chain reaches eleven jurisdictions, but only five hold more than one provision in every area and, outside the EU, no jurisdiction imposes a binding evaluation duty on a model developer. The dataset gives researchers and policymakers a provision-level basis for identifying where regimes could align, and where coordination would have to start from scratch.

</details>


### [51] [Multi-Tier Mentorship with AI-Assisted Development: Authentic Engineering for K-12 and Undergraduates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19379)
*Kelly Yuan,Ronald Liu,Daniel Crawford,Weihao Qu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多层级导师制框架，通过AI辅助开发工具连接高中生与大学生，让高中生担任产品负责人、本科生担任技术架构师、教师作为低干预顾问，并通过LuckyTag（基于NFC的失物招领系统）验证该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12学生有创造性工程想法但缺乏技术技能，而大学生有编码专长但缺少领导真实项目或指导他人的机会。AI辅助工具的快速发展为连接这两个群体提供了可能，但有效的K-12与大学协作结构尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出多层级导师制框架：高中生作为产品负责人、本科生作为技术架构师、教师作为最小干预顾问。通过LuckyTag（隐私保护的NFC失物招领系统）进行测试，采用调查数据和主题分析评估效果。

Result: 试点实验（4名高中生、3名本科生、2名教师）的调查数据显示感知障碍显著降低，系统架构理解有所提升。主题分析表明AI放大了而非取代了导师需求，在逻辑和安全性方面仍需人类监督。

Conclusion: 提出一种公平的K-12与大学计算整合协作混合模型，强调"AI微观管理"和架构推理优于传统语法教学，为跨学段协作提供了新路径。

Abstract: K-12 students often possess creative engineering ideas but lack technical skills to build them, while undergraduates have coding expertise but few opportunities to lead real-world projects or mentor others. The rapid development of AI-assisted tools offers a potential bridge to connect these groups, yet the structure for effective K-12 and university collaborations remains underexplored. This paper introduces a multi-tiered mentorship framework enabling high school students to engage in authentic engineering through AI-assisted development using large language models and AI agents, while undergraduate mentors provide architectural oversight. We test this framework through LuckyTag, a privacy-preserving NFC-based lost-and-found system. The model positions high schoolers as product leads, undergraduates as technical architects, and faculty as minimal-intervention advisors. A pilot with four high school students, three undergraduates and two faculty yielded survey data showing high perceived barrier removal and gains in system architecture understanding. Thematic analysis reveals that AI amplifies rather than supplants mentoring demands, requiring human oversight for logic and security. These findings suggest a hybrid model for equitable K-12 and university collaboration on computing integration that emphasizes "AI micromanagement" and architectural reasoning over traditional syntax.

</details>


### [52] [Navigating Epistemic Monocultures in AI-Driven Science: A Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19390)
*Sina Fazelpour,Joseph O'Brien,Hannah Rubin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过NK适应度景观模型研究AI融入科学研究社区的影响，发现非个性化AI系统仅在特定条件下有益，而个性化AI通过增强多样性能在更广范围内带来收益，但需配套的制度适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI融入科学研究承诺加速发现，但可能引发科学社区的趋同化（homogenization）风险。作者希望系统性地探索这些承诺与风险，评估不同AI部署策略对科学多样性和发现效率的影响。

Method: 采用NK适应度景观模型（NK landscape model）模拟研究社区的探索过程，对比三种AI干预方式：非个性化统一指导、随机化干预、个性化干预，并考察不同问题结构、实践模式和基线研究能力下的表现。

Result: 非个性化AI系统仅在问题结构、实践模式和基线能力三者狭窄的组合下有益，否则有害；随机化仅在可分解问题上有效；个性化能提升多样性，在更广条件下带来收益，但这些收益依赖有效的制度适应（新标准与实践）。

Conclusion: AI对科学社区的影响并非天然有益，个性化是较有前景的方向，但必须与制度创新相结合，否则收益无法自动实现。研究为AI在科学中的应用提供了重要的设计启示。

Abstract: AI integration into scientific communities promises accelerated discovery but raises concerns about detrimental homogenization. We develop an NK landscape model to explore these promises and risks. We find that non-personalized AI systems that offer uniform guidance yield benefits only under a narrow conjunction of problem structure, practices, and baseline research capabilities, becoming harmful otherwise. We implement two proposed mitigations: randomization and personalization. While randomization's utility remains restricted to decomposable problems, personalization can enhance diversity, enabling benefits across a broader range of conditions. Crucially, these benefits are not automatic, but depend on effective institutional adaptation, requiring new standards and practices.

</details>


### [53] [A Scoping Review of Methods to Measure the Energy and Carbon Footprint of Web Tracking and Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19495)
*Nils Bonfils,Christoph Becker*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文献综述系统梳理了网络跟踪与广告能耗/碳足迹测量方法，从46篇初筛文献中筛选出15篇，归纳出五种方法论途径：广告拦截、受控环境、广告重放、流量分析及文献推导估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICT行业碳足迹持续上升，网络跟踪和广告对环境的影响日益受到关注，但相关研究分散于不同学科，术语不一致，缺乏系统性的方法论梳理，难以支撑全面的环境核算。

Method: 采用范围综述（scoping review）方法，通过Google Scholar基于标题的结构化搜索获得46篇初始文献，经过筛选最终纳入15篇论文，并从中识别归纳出五种不同的方法论途径。

Result: 从文献中识别出五种测量网络跟踪与广告能耗/碳足迹的方法论途径：广告拦截（ad blocking）、受控环境（controlled environment）、广告重放（replaying ads）、流量分析（traffic flow analysis）以及文献推导估算（literature-derived estimation）。

Conclusion: 该综述为当前方法论格局提供了结构化认识，为广告技术生态系统的更全面环境核算奠定了基础，有助于推动该领域术语和方法的统一与规范化。

Abstract: The environmental impact of web tracking and advertising is increasingly receiving attention as the ICT sector's carbon footprint keeps rising. Yet the scholarship addressing this question remains scattered across disciplines and inconsistent in its terminology. This paper presents a scoping review of the literature on methods for measuring the energy and carbon footprint of web tracking and advertising. From an initial pool of 46 articles identified through a structured title-based search on Google Scholar, we arrived at a final corpus of 15 papers, from which we identified five distinct methodological approaches: ad blocking, controlled environment, replaying ads, traffic flow analysis, and literature-derived estimation. This review provides a structured overview of the current methodological landscape and a foundation for more comprehensive environmental accounting of the ad tech ecosystem.

</details>


### [54] [Two-sided receptivity to conversational AI agents in online dating: Bilingual survey data from Fledge.Love](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19545)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文发布了两份来自在线约会平台Fledge.Love的匿名化调查数据集（N=2,617和N=2,894），分别测量用户对自主对话代理的接受度（区分部署自身代理和遇到他人代理两种角色）以及对三种被动生成式AI功能的兴趣。数据集附带模型评分、双语代码手册、k-匿名审计的匿名化流程、可执行的笔记本和标准输出，支持人机通信、推荐系统和跨文化技术接受度研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主对话代理和生成式AI功能正被快速添加到在线约会平台中，但公众对其态度的实证证据积累滞后，尤其缺乏从接收方视角出发的研究——即当用户遇到的资料、消息或对话伙伴是机器生成时，人们作何反应。

Method: 在Fledge.Love平台（服务国际用户）上收集了两个匿名化调查数据集：数据集一（N=2,617）使用七项量表测量对自主对话代理的接受度，区分主体角色（部署自己的代理）与对方角色（遇到他人的代理），同时包含六个有序协变量和两个辅助项目；数据集二（N=2,894）测量对三种被动生成式AI功能的兴趣。发布内容涵盖2,499个完整案例的模型派生分数、双语代码手册、带有k-匿名审计的文档化匿名化流程、可执行分析笔记本及标准输出。

Result: 提供了两个大规模真实用户调查数据集，涵盖俄语和英语两种语言版本，并包含完整的数据处理文档（匿名化、k-匿名审计、模型评分）和可复现的分析工具链。

Conclusion: 该数据发布填补了约会平台中AI生成内容接收方态度的证据空白，为多领域（人机通信、推荐系统、跨文化技术接受度）的后续研究提供了可重复使用的资源。

Abstract: Autonomous conversational agents and generative-AI features are being added to online dating platforms faster than public evidence about user attitudes can accumulate, and the scarcest evidence concerns the receiving side: how people react when the profiles, messages, or conversation partners they encounter are machine-generated. We release two anonymized survey datasets collected from active users of Fledge.Love, a dating platform serving an international user base. The first (N = 2,617; Russian and English forms) measures receptivity to autonomous conversational agents with a seven-item battery that separates the principal role (deploying one's own agent) from the counterpart role (encountering someone else's), plus six ordinal covariates and two auxiliary items. The second (N = 2,894) measures interest in three passive generative-AI features. The release includes model-derived scores for 2,499 complete cases, a bilingual codebook, a documented anonymization pipeline with a k-anonymity audit, executable analysis notebooks, and canonical outputs, supporting reuse in human-AI communication, recommender-systems, and cross-cultural technology-acceptance research.

</details>


### [55] [Modeling AI Overreliance as a Complex Adaptive System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19616)
*Ahana Biswas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文将AI依赖建模为群体过程，通过贝叶斯信念更新与同伴学习，揭示任务难度与AI质量设定基准、社会学习产生共识而非过度依赖、社会证明引发依赖的反馈级联、反馈设计可防止群体校准崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助对人群的影响取决于人们是否恰当依赖AI（正确时信任、错误时核查），但现有研究仅从个体层面研究依赖，忽略了群体层面的社会互动过程。

Method: 将AI依赖建模为群体过程：智能体反复独立求解任务、接受AI答案或验证AI答案，通过贝叶斯信念更新对AI质量的认知；在网络化情境中，智能体还向同伴学习。结合均值保持定理与2×2拓扑×标签设计实验验证。

Result: 四项结果：环境（任务难度与AI质量）设定过度依赖与校准遗憾的基线；社会学习产生共识而非过度依赖（均值保持定理证实）；社会证明使依赖变成反馈级联，可见的未验证使用抑制验证；使验证可见或抑制社会证明可逆转群体校准崩溃。

Conclusion: 将AI依赖重新框定为计算社会动力学问题，个体学习、同伴观察与反馈暴露共同决定群体能否保持校准。

Abstract: Whether AI assistance helps or harms a population depends less on the model's accuracy than on whether people rely on it appropriately trusting it when it is right and checking it when it is not. Yet reliance is usually studied one user at a time. We model it as a population process: agents repeatedly solve a task alone, accept an AI answer, or verify it, updating a Bayesian belief about AI quality and, when networked, learning from peers. Four results form one story. The environment sets the baseline: task difficulty and AI quality fix both overreliance and calibration regret. Social learning creates consensus, not overreliance: a mean-preservation theorem, confirmed by a 2*2 topology*tagging design, shows connectivity moves the aggregate only when influence transmits beliefs. Social proof turns reliance into a feedback cascade: visible unverified use suppresses verification and tips the population into collective overreliance. Feedback design can prevent collapse: making verification visible or dampening social proof reverses it. Together, the results frame AI reliance as a computational social dynamics problem, where individual learning, peer observation, and feedback exposure jointly shape whether a population remains calibrated.

</details>


### [56] [ChatGPT Solves All Tested Qiskit Homework Assignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19707)
*Alexei Kaltchenko,Gurnivaj Tiwana*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在量子软件教育中，生成式AI是否使可自动评分的Qiskit作业失效。作者设计了三个个性化的、需实际运行的作业包（含种子电路、QFT恢复、Deutsch-Jozsa），每个包在50次ChatGPT会话中测试共150次，发现所有最终产物均能通过评分器，零次需要人工修正，表明当前设计对ChatGPT缺乏弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（如ChatGPT）使学生能够将量子编程作业直接交给AI完成，这对基于自动评分的量子软件课程构成了重大评估挑战。作者希望探讨：是否能在不禁止AI的前提下，通过设计要求学生实际运行、审查和讨论结果来维持作业的评估有效性。

Method: 设计了三个Qiskit作业包：(1) 带比特翻转和定制测量映射的种子基态电路；(2) QFT及逆变换恢复；(3) 带定制oracle掩码的种子Deutsch-Jozsa算法。设计包含个性化、模拟器执行、JSON提交、隐藏参考、电路度量、反思和可选的IBM Quantum运行。每个作业包的学生可见实例在50个独立ChatGPT会话中测试，共150个会话，评估最终工件是否通过评分器及是否需要人工干预。

Result: 150次ChatGPT会话中，所有最终工件均成功执行并通过评分器。9个会话被完整归档，均无需操作员修改代码或修正量子逻辑。按照操作定义，每个测试实例的ChatGPT弹性为零。

Conclusion: 个性化的、面向执行的take-home设计无法有效防止学生通过AI完成作业。原因是：种子只改变参数而非任务结构、预期结果可从可见作业逻辑推导、脚手架暴露了关键解决步骤、隐藏评分只验证输出一致性而未验证独立写作或理解。建议将正确工件与直接评估（监督修改、口头答辩、预测和迁移任务）相结合。

Abstract: Generative AI creates an assessment challenge in quantum software education: a student can provide a homework notebook to ChatGPT and request a completed submission. This study examined whether introductory Qiskit homework could remain autogradable while requiring students to run, review, and discuss results rather than banning AI. Three packages were tested: seeded basis-state circuits with bit flips and customized measurement mappings; Quantum Fourier Transform followed by inverse-transform recovery; and seeded Deutsch-Jozsa with customized oracle masks. The designs used personalization, simulator execution, JSON submissions, hidden references, circuit metrics, reflections, and optional IBM Quantum execution. For each package, one student-visible instance was tested in 50 separate ChatGPT sessions, yielding 150 sessions overall. Every final artifact was executed and passed its grader. Nine sessions were fully archived; none required operator code changes or correction of quantum logic. Under the study's operational definition, each tested instance had zero observed ChatGPT-resiliency. Seeds changed parameters rather than task structure, expected results remained derivable from visible assignment logic, scaffolding exposed key solution steps, and hidden grading verified output consistency without establishing independent authorship or understanding. Because one instance was repeated for each package, the results do not establish solvability for every seed or possible Qiskit assessment. The tested personalized, execution-oriented take-home designs therefore did not prevent successful completion under a minimally engaged-student workflow. Correct artifacts should be complemented by direct assessment through supervised modification, oral defense, prediction, and transfer tasks.

</details>


### [57] [Understanding as an Explicit and Assessable Component of Frontier AI Safety Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19816)
*Stephen Barrett,Robin Bloomfield,Alexandra Chirilă,Mamoon Masud,David Meredith Hardy,Phillip Mulvana*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使AI系统决策者的"理解"显式化且可评估的临时方法论，要求对四个理解对象（决策、决策框架、安全论证、系统情境）进行显式描述并论证其充分性，同时提供机制来评估决策者对此理解的表征是否充分。该方法基于Assurance 2.0框架，将Elgin和Arendt的哲学理解观操作化。通过两个场景的试验验证了方法论的可行性和生成性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI部署决策日益在时间压力下进行，且随着AI生成制品的使用，传统的安全案例（safety cases）和系统卡片（system cards）可能不再足以证明决策者具备充分理解。决策者需要充分理解复杂AI系统才能做出良好决策，但目前缺乏使理解显式化并可评估的方法论。

Method: 提出临时方法论：1) 要求对四个理解对象（决策、决策框架、安全论证、系统情境）进行显式描述；2) 论证这些理解是否充分；3) 提供机制来描述和评估决策者对理解的表征是否充分。该方法论基于Assurance 2.0框架，操作化了Elgin和Arendt的哲学理解理论。通过两个场景试验评估：一个是在机器人公司部署AI编码代理时关于scheming风险的基于角色分析场景，另一个是更高不确定性和决策关键性的'If Anyone Builds It, Everyone Dies'论证场景。

Result: 在两个试验场景中，方法论均能成功应用，并且被发现具有生成性（generative）：用于论证理解充分性的分析（内部一致性、锚定、合适的错误、外部一致性）实际上驱动了工程实践。

Conclusion: 该方法论可行且具有生成价值——理解充分性的论证分析不仅用于事后验证，还能在决策过程中发挥驱动工程设计和决策的作用，为高不确定性和高风险场景下的AI部署决策提供了新的评估工具。

Abstract: Decision makers need sufficient understanding to make good decisions about complex AI systems. However, AI deployment decisions are increasingly made under time-pressure, and this combined with the use of AI generated artefact creation, can mean that the existence of safety cases and system cards may no longer demonstrate that sufficient understanding exists. Our provisional methodology for making understanding explicit and assessable requires the production of an explicit description of 4 objects of understanding (decision, decision-frame, safety justification, system-in-context) and a justification for the adequacy of this understanding. In addition, the methodology provides a mechanism for describing and evaluating the adequacy of the decision-maker representation of this understanding. It builds on recent developments in safety cases using the Assurance 2.0 framework to operationalise the philosophical basis of understanding from Elgin and Arendt. To assess the methodology we trialled two different scenarios. One scenario, which we investigated through role-based analysis, concerned the risk of scheming in the deployment of an AI coding agent in a robotics company and the other scenario was for the higher uncertainty, more decision-critical argument of 'If Anyone Builds It, Everyone Dies' (Yudkowsky and Soares). The trial's central finding, for these two scenarios, is that the methodology could be applied and was found to be generative: we found the analyses that justify sufficiency of understanding (internal coherence, tethering, felicitous falsehoods, external coherence) drives the engineering.

</details>

Result: 提取的符号定价公式在90,000个完全独立样本上实现0.483%的平均相对定价误差。单调性约束训练目标具有收敛性保证，所学模型能够保持真实定价映射的单调性质。

Abstract: We study the approximation of CAT bond prices under a compound Poisson loss model with lognormal severities using Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs). Building on a baseline-plus-residual learning pipeline, we train a KAN on the deviation from a closed-form lognormal baseline price and extract an interpretable symbolic pricing formula. The extracted formula achieves an average relative pricing error of 0.483% on a fully disjoint holdout sample of 90,000 simulated prices. In contrast to purely empirical modelling, we also analyse structural properties of the true CAT bond pricing map, including monotonicity with respect to the catastrophe arrival intensity lambda, the initial short rate r0, and the trigger threshold D. We derive sufficient conditions on KAN edge functions that guarantee these monotonicities are preserved by the learned model, and formulate a monotonicity-constrained training objective with a convergence guarantee. Our results suggest that symbolic KAN surrogates provide a practical compromise between accuracy, computational speed, and interpretability for CAT bond valuation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市场数据只揭示一条已实现的轨迹，许多重要问题本质上是反事实的，需要真实的轨迹级模拟。然而现有金融生成模型往往孤立地建模订单事件或市场状态（如限价订单簿），忽视了订单流与流动性之间的动态交互，无法准确模拟市场微观结构。

Method: M3是一个状态-事件生成式基础模型，将订单事件与限价订单簿流动性状态联合建模，学习生成未来的订单流轨迹，同时捕捉事件与状态之间的动态交互关系。模型在大规模真实订单级股票市场数据上进行训练。

Result: M3展现出可预测的扩展（scaling）行为，能够复现关键的市场经验事实（stylized facts），并成功支持预测、压力测试和市场影响分析等实际模拟应用。

Conclusion: M3的结果表明，存在一种可扩展的基础模型范式，能够在微观结构层面实现反事实市场模拟，为金融市场仿真提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Market microstructure simulation aims to model how liquidity, prices, and order flow evolve in electronic financial markets. Since market data reveal only one realized trajectory, many important questions are inherently counterfactual and require realistic trajectory-level simulation. Existing financial generative models, however, often model order events and market states, such as the LOB, in isolation, overlooking the dynamic interaction between order flow and liquidity in market microstructure. We propose the \textbf{M3} (\underline{M}arket \underline{M}icrostructure \underline{M}odel), a state-event generative foundation model for market microstructure dynamics. \textbf{M3} learns to generate future order-flow trajectories, while accounting for the evolving interaction between order events and limit-order-book liquidity. Trained on large-scale order-level real stock market data, \textbf{M3} exhibits predictable scaling behavior, reproduces key market stylized facts, and enables practical simulation-based applications including forecasting, stress testing, and market-impact analysis. These results suggest a scalable foundation-model paradigm for counterfactual market simulation at the microstructure level.

</details>


### [60] [Deep-MKV-TS: Path-Dependent McKean--Vlasov Control for Financial Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19394)
*Samer El Boustany,Théo Basseras,Samy Mekkaoui,Alexandre Alouadi,Yadh Hafsi,Huyên Pham*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep-MKV-TS，一个基于路径依赖McKean-Vlasov框架的金融情景生成方法。该方法通过匹配生成情景与真实数据的路径和波动率特征，保留可解释参考模型的漂移项并调整其波动率，利用神经网络和样本化随机最大值原理求解控制问题。实验表明该方法在Heston、Heston-mixture等模型上显著减少参考模型的路径依赖和波动率缺陷，并在股指期货日内数据上改善条件预测，优于直接训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融情景生成模型（如Heston模型）在路径依赖和波动率特征上存在不足，而完全灵活的生成模型缺乏可解释性。作者希望在不舍弃可解释参考模型的前提下，通过控制其波动率来修正缺陷，从而实现可解释性与灵活性的平衡。

Method: Deep-MKV-TS框架：（1）从可解释的参考模型出发，保留其漂移项，仅调整波动率；（2）通过匹配路径特征和波动率特征与真实数据一致来定义随机动态；（3）加入正则化惩罚以防止过度偏离参考动力学；（4）使用神经网络实现的样本化随机最大值原理求解该控制问题。

Result: 在Heston及Heston-mixture模型上显著减少参考模型的路径依赖和波动率缺陷；在延迟波动率实验中，校正效果随预测视界增加仍保持有效，而直接训练方法变得不可靠；在留出的股指期货日内数据上改善条件预测，达到与灵活生成模型和历史基线相当的性能；在固定回撤风险目标下支持更大风险敞口。

Conclusion: 路径依赖的McKean-Vlasov控制可以丰富可解释参考模型而不必替换它，为金融情景生成提供了一种兼顾可解释性与灵活性的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Deep-MKV-TS, a path-dependent McKean-Vlasov framework for financial scenario generation. The stochastic dynamics are chosen by matching selected path and volatility features of generated scenarios to those observed in the data. Starting from an interpretable reference model, Deep-MKV-TS preserves the reference drift and adjusts its volatility, while a regularization penalty limits unnecessary departures from the calibrated dynamics. We solve the resulting control problem using a neural, sample-based implementation of the stochastic maximum principle.
  We validate the method against an exactly computable oracle. On Heston and Heston-mixture models, Deep-MKV-TS substantially reduces path-dependent and volatility-related deficiencies of the reference model. In delayed-volatility experiments, the correction remains effective as the forecasting horizon increases, while direct training becomes less reliable. On held-out intraday equity-index futures, the corrected model improves conditional forecasts relative to the reference and reaches a level of performance comparable to flexible generative and historical baselines. The resulting scenarios also support greater exposure than the reference under a fixed drawdown-risk target. These results show that path-dependent McKean-Vlasov control can enrich an interpretable reference model without replacing it.

</details>

### [61] [Concentrated Liquidity Provision: a Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19389)
*Georgios Chionas,Charalampos Kleitsikas,Stefanos Leonardos,Leandro Sánchez-Betancourt,Carmine Ventre*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文研究Uniswap V3这类集中流动性AMM中流动性提供者(LP)的动态做市问题，将其建模为随机脉冲控制问题，并用强化学习求解，得到可解释的策略，能根据错价、再平衡成本、不确定性、库存暴露和风险偏好等因素动态分配流动性，有效压缩PnL分布的左尾，避免高不确定性下的灾难性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Uniswap V3等集中流动性AMM市场中，LP面临顺序决策问题：需要决定何时再平衡头寸以及如何选择价格区间来配置资本。这个问题具有动态性和复杂性，需要一个系统化的方法来解决。

Method: 将动态流动性提供建模为随机脉冲控制问题，使用强化学习(RL)求解，并重点关注解的可解释性。

Result: 学习到的策略表现出丰富的状态依赖行为，能根据错价、再平衡成本、不确定性、库存暴露和异质风险偏好来分配流动性。这些行为压缩了PnL分布的左尾，避免高不确定性下的灾难性结果。

Conclusion: RL代理与AMM微观结构文献中的基线和复杂代理进行基准比较，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Automated market makers (AMMs) are a cornerstone of decentralised finance (DeFi). Constant product markets with concentrated liquidity, such as UniswapV3, are now a well-established design. In these markets, liquidity providers (LPs) face a sequential decision problem: they must decide when to rebalance their positions and which price ranges to allocate capital to as market conditions evolve. We formulate dynamic liquidity provision as a stochastic impulse control problem and use reinforcement learning (RL) to solve it, focusing on providing interpretable solutions. We show that learned policies exhibit rich state-dependent behaviour, allocating liquidity according to mispricing, rebalancing costs, uncertainty, inventory exposure, and heterogeneous risk preferences. These behaviours help compress the left tail of the Profit and Loss (PnL) distribution and avoid catastrophic outcomes under high uncertainty. Finally, we benchmark the RL agents against baseline and sophisticated agents from the AMM microstructure literature and analyse their performance.

</details>

### [62] [Calibration-Induced Degeneracy in LLM Financial Forecasting: An Audit-Trailed Case Study on Next-Day Market Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20304)
*Arin Mohanty*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了一种"校准导致的退化"现象：昂贵的LLM特征只有在校准允许其影响预测时才有效。在两只大盘基金的次日风险研究中，2022年校准将所有四个LLM权重设为零，导致后续856个评分无法影响评估。相比之下，近乎零成本的头条计数显著降低了SPY方差预测损失（0.001720，95%族系区间[0.000719, 0.002830]）。作者提出"校准可行性检查点"：在获取留出特征前，先拟合映射、扰动特征并检查预测响应是否显著，该检查无需留出结果即可提前终止付费的全历史推理阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昂贵的LLM特征只有在校准允许其影响预测时才真正有用。作者发现校准与特征利用之间存在脱节——校准可能将所有LLM权重设为0，使后续昂贵的推理工作完全白费。这暴露了特征获取前缺乏可行性验证的问题。

Method: 在两只大盘基金的次日风险研究中，对比全历史评分的LLM特征与低成本头条计数。校准允许符号权重激活全部四个LLM映射，同时提出"校准可行性检查点"：拟合映射后扰动特征，要求预测有实质响应才获取留出特征，此检查不依赖留出结果。

Result: 全历史评分后，2022年校准将所有LLM权重设为零（校准诱导退化），使856个后续评分无效。允许符号权重虽重新激活全部映射，但族系校正后均未改善预测。相反，低成本头条计数将SPY方差预测损失降低0.001720（族系区间[0.000719, 0.002830]），显著优于LLM特征。

Conclusion: 低成本基线是评估昂贵LLM特征的关键诊断工具。校准可行性检查点能在不依赖留出结果的情况下，提前识别LLM特征无法影响预测的情形，从而避免在无效推理阶段浪费资源。

Abstract: Costly LLM features matter only if calibration lets them affect the forecast. We document a failure of this link in a next-day risk study of two broad-market funds. Full-history scoring preceded the 2022 calibration. Calibration then set all four LLM weights to zero. The 856 later scores therefore could not affect the evaluation. We call this calibration-induced degeneracy. Allowing signed weights reactivated all four mappings. None improved forecasts after familywise correction. By contrast, a near-zero-cost headline count reduced SPY variance-forecast loss by 0.001720 (95 percent familywise interval: [0.000719, 0.002830]). The cheap baseline is therefore a critical diagnostic. We propose a calibration-viability checkpoint. Fit the mapping, perturb the feature over prespecified calibration values, and require a meaningful forecast response before acquiring holdout features. The check uses no holdout outcomes. Here, it would have stopped the paid full-history inference phase.

</details>

### [63] [The Reconfiguration Premium: Co-movement Structure as an Unspanned Dimension of the Variance Risk Premium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20020)
*Lucas Carvalho*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文研究S&P 500股票相关性矩阵主特征空间随时间变化的"转向率"，发现该转向率具有定价能力，并与聚合方差风险溢价紧密耦合（t=5.40），且仅持久成分被定价，其机制是预付（prepayment）：隐含方差即时上升，波动率滞后2-3个季度跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对冲比率、因子模型和分散化组合都依赖于对企业共同变动的估计，但该估计不稳定：企业会在市场视为一致的分组间迁移，当迁移足够多时横截面的组织轴就会转向。作者希望量化这一转向速率并检验其是否被定价。

Method: 以连续12个月S&P 500相关矩阵的次优势特征空间之间的主角度均方正弦来度量转向率。将该速率与聚合方差风险溢价进行回归（t=5.40），并检验水平测量、隐含相关曲面、持久/非持久成分分解，以及预付机制（VAR或脉冲响应：隐含方差即时反应，波动率滞后2-3季度）。

Result: 典型月份重写五分之一的横截面结构并保留五分之四。该速率与聚合方差风险溢价耦合于t=5.40，无水平测量相关超过0.32，隐含相关曲面最多覆盖其6.7%。仅持久成分被定价；预付机制成立，模拟零假设下p<0.03（h=1-9）。三个预注册边界均成立：无择时alpha、无崩盘保护、下行版本与强度不可分。

Conclusion: 方差风险溢价部分是对在地图仍被重绘时持有风险暴露的租金。市场结构本身的重新组织速率是一种被定价的风险维度，且溢价补偿的是修订的速度而非距离。

Abstract: Hedge ratios, factor models and diversified portfolios all rest on an estimate of which firms move together. That estimate is not stable: firms migrate between the groupings the market treats as coherent, and when enough migrate the organizing axes of the cross-section turn. We measure the rate of that turning as the mean squared sine of the principal angles between subdominant eigenspaces of consecutive twelve-month S&P 500 correlation matrices. A typical month rewrites a fifth of the structure and carries four-fifths forward. That rate is priced: it couples to the aggregate variance risk premium at t = 5.40, no level measure correlates above 0.32, and the implied-correlation surface spans at most 6.7 percent of it. Only the persistent component is priced - the premium compensates the pace of revision, not the distance traveled. The mechanism is prepayment: implied variance rises on impact, volatility follows two to three quarters later (simulated-null p < 0.03 at h = 1-9), and the premium converges as it arrives. Three pre-registered boundaries hold: no timing alpha, no crash protection, and a downside version inseparable from intensity. The premium is, in part, rent on exposure held over a map still being redrawn.

</details>

### [64] [Filtering Credit Risk with Stochastic Discontinuities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19221)
*Félix B. Tambe-Ndonfack*

Main category: q-fin.PR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个不完全信息下的结构化信用风险模型，投资者仅能通过含噪声的市场信号和预定企业披露间接观察企业价值。披露日期已知但其信息内容是随机的，导致观测过程出现随机不连续性。作者通过带可预测跳跃的非线性滤波框架推导了Kushner-Stratonovich方程，并基于局部风险最小化研究了部分信息下违约敏感证券的定价与对冲问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结构化信用风险模型通常假设投资者能完全观测企业价值，或仅考虑不可达跳跃带来的信息不确定。然而现实中，企业定期披露（如财报）是计划内事件，其信息内容未知，会在披露日造成观测过程的随机间断。这种可预测跳跃对信用风险定价与对冲的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 将非线性滤波框架推广到带可预测跳跃的情形，推导出结构化信用风险模型（含内生违约）的Kushner-Stratonovich方程。基于该滤波结果，利用局部风险最小化方法研究部分信息下违约敏感证券（如CDS）的定价与对冲，并通过数值实验分析披露事件的影响。

Result: 可预测披露事件与内生违约之间存在交互作用，导致条件违约补偿子产生离散调整，形成公告驱动的信用利差和套期比率扭曲——这些现象在经典扩散模型和不可达跳跃模型中都不存在。数值实验表明，预定披露显著影响滤波违约概率、CDS利差和套期策略，并在信用利差中产生特征性的公告前动态。

Conclusion: 预定披露事件是信用风险市场中不可忽视的信息结构特征，会导致与传统模型定性不同的定价和套期行为。本文为理解公告驱动型信用利差波动提供了理论与数值框架。

Abstract: We develop a structural credit-risk model under incomplete information in which investors observe firm value only indirectly through noisy market signals and scheduled corporate disclosures. While disclosure dates are known in advance, their informational content is random, leading to stochastic discontinuities in the observation process. We derive the Kushner-Stratonovich equation for structural credit-risk models with endogenous default by applying the nonlinear filtering framework with predictable jumps. We then study the valuation and local risk-minimization hedging for default-sensitive securities under partial information. The interaction between predictable disclosure events and endogenous default produces discrete adjustments in the conditional default compensator, leading to announcement-driven distortions in credit spreads and hedge ratios that are absent from classical diffusion-based and inaccessible-jump models. Numerical experiments illustrate how scheduled disclosures affect filtered default probabilities, Credit Default Swaps (CDS) spreads, and hedging strategies, generating characteristic pre-announcement dynamics in credit spreads.

</details>


### [65] [Henstock--Kurzweil Path Integral in Financial Mathematics: A Machine-Verified Pricing of European and Barrier Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19223)
*Alexander S. Ushakov,Yury N. Berdinsky*

Main category: q-fin.PR

TL;DR: 本文使用Henstock-Kurzweil规范积分直接处理Black-Scholes期权定价模型，绕过随机微积分，从高斯圆柱核推导出欧式看涨期权价格，并在Lean 4/Mathlib中完成了机器可检查的形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Black-Scholes定价依赖于Ito随机积分和张量分析，证明过程复杂且难以形式化。作者希望展示HK规范积分可以更直接、更简洁地处理期权定价问题，并实现机器可验证的证明。

Method: 在风险中性测度下，利用对数价格作为带漂移nu = r - sigma^2/2的布朗运动，将其转移密度表示为高斯核G_t。使用HK规范积分验证Chapman-Kolmogorov半群性质、概率密度性质、定价算子的强连续性，并推导出Black-Scholes闭式解。整个证明在Lean 4/Mathlib中完成，仅依赖propext、Classical.choice和Quot.sound，无任何sorry占位。

Result: 成功获得欧式看涨期权闭式解C = S_0 N(d_1) - K e^{-rT} N(d_2)，并在Lean 4中完成了所有关键性质的"sorry"-free正式化证明。数字期权和障碍期权作为进一步例子证明了方法的普适性，同时验证了该方法与经典Ito微积分在连续极限下相容。

Conclusion: HK规范积分为期权定价提供了一种无需随机微积分的替代路径，且该路径完全可形式化验证。这一方法不仅适用于欧式期权，还能推广到数字期权和障碍期权，展现出广泛的适用性和理论优雅性。

Abstract: We apply the Henstock--Kurzweil (HK) gauge integral to the Black--Scholes model of option pricing and obtain the European call price directly from a Gaussian cylindrical kernel, without stochastic calculus. Under the risk- neutral measure, the log-price is a Brownian motion with drift nu = r - sigma^2/2. Its transition density is the Gaussian kernel G_t(x,y) = (2 pi sigma^2 t)^{-1/2} exp( - (y - x - nu t)^2 / (2 sigma^2 t) ). We give a machine-checked formalization in Lean 4 / Mathlib of the following: the Chapman--Kolmogorov (semigroup) property, the fact that G_t is a probability density, strong continuity of the pricing operator, the closed-form price C = S_0 N(d_1) - K e^{-rT} N(d_2) with the standard normal CDF N, and the exactness of the drift--diffusion Chernoff splitting at every level. The entire proof is "sorry"-free and depends only on propext, Classical.choice, and Quot.sound. Digital and barrier options are treated as further examples, illustrating the universality of the method, and we show that the construction is compatible with the classical Ito calculus in the continuum limit.

</details>

Abstract: Accelerating the electrification of thermal energy requires vapor-compression heat pumps capable of dynamic, grid-responsive operation. However, equipment engineering remains fragmented across static rating-point selection, stiff multi-phase transient simulation, and gradient-based optimal control. Here, we present an end-to-end differentiable, finite-volume vapor-compression framework implemented natively in JAX that automates machine sizing directly from stated thermal duties and unifies dynamic simulation with predictive control under a single compiled residual $\dot{y}={f}(t,{y},{u})$. Thermodynamic evaluations bypass runtime root-finding via bilinear $(p,h)$ manifolds pre-flashed from Helmholtz equations of state, enabling analytical forward-mode automatic differentiation. Mass conservation across multi-phase coils is strictly preserved by incorporating both $(\partialρ/\partial p)_h$ and $(\partialρ/\partial h)_p$ partial derivatives into the dynamic pressure differential equation. The sizer directly inverts compressor displacement, electronic expansion valve area, and heat-exchanger tube counts via four-point cycle synthesis and $\varepsilon$-NTU matching using the identical polytropic compressor map. Crucially, the compiled physics kernel is shared symmetrically between $L$-stable TR-BDF2 stiff integration and implicit-Euler Model Predictive Control (MPC), eliminating plant-controller surrogate mismatch. Validated against open-access experimental benchmarks without parameter fitting, the framework predicts cooling capacity with $7.37\%$ MAPE across 16 mini-split operational runs and bounds on-period cooling error within $1.19\%$--$1.62\%$ on utility-scale Hardware-in-the-Loop traces. This work provides an open-source, differentiable foundation for automated machine synthesis, dynamic grid orchestration, and gradient-based hardware-control co-design.

</details>


### [67] [The Calibration Illusion in Traffic Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19642)
*Cameron Hickert,Maryam Samaei,Athena Wang,Chengyuan Zhang,Lijun Sun,Yanbing Wang,Mostafa Ameli,Cathy Wu*

Main category: eess.SY

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人工校准耗时且主观，因此交通领域转向自动化校准方法。然而这些"自动"方法背后隐藏了大量未量化的定制化手工工作，这种"校准幻觉"阻碍了客观比较、损害了可复现性并导致研究碎片化。

Method: 提出一个全面的基准测试（benchmark），设计用于同时暴露公路微观仿真的校准幻觉，并为校准方法的比较提供统一的"共同标尺"。涵盖多种场景，并对大规模公路校准与传统简化设置中的误差来源进行了额外实验分析。

Result: 在多种场景下的结果建立了算法在无需定制调参情况下能达到的新基线，揭示了当前研究差距，并为校准的累积科学提供了工具。额外实验阐明了大规模公路校准错误相对于常用的简化设置所特有的来源。

Conclusion: 自动校准是一种幻觉，需要客观基准来推动可复现、可比较的校准科学。本文的基准填补了这一空白，既曝光了问题，也提供了促进该领域累积科学进步的公共工具。

Abstract: The transportation community seeks to use calibration methods for highway traffic microsimulation. This is a response to the time-consuming and subjective nature of traditional manual calibration, as well as the growing prevalence of data for calibration. This work argues that this "automatic" calibration is largely an illusion. A significant - and unquantified - amount of bespoke manual work is hidden behind these methods. This illusion inhibits a core component of scientific advancement: objective comparison against a shared standard. This impedes evaluation, undermines reproducibility, and fragments research. To address this gap, this paper introduces a comprehensive benchmark designed to simultaneously expose the calibration illusion for highway microsimulation and provide a common ruler. The results across a range of scenarios present a new baseline for what the algorithms can achieve without bespoke tuning, revealing the research gap that remains and providing a tool to advance a cumulative science of calibration. Additional experiments provide insights into the source of calibration errors that arise in large-scale highway calibration relative to the simplified settings under which methods are commonly developed.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该方法将距离继电器故障检测形式化为可达集问题，通过降阶二维模型解决了计算难题，为距离保护提供了理论严谨且计算高效的故障检测框架。

Abstract: Distance relays detect faults on transmission lines from local voltage and current measurements. In this paper, we characterize the set of time-domain measurements a distance relay can observe during a fault as a reachable set. We define corresponding fault tests in terms of set-based state estimation. This allows us to model the full network as an RLC circuit with voltage and current sources. In general, computing reachable sets and set-based state estimates is intractable. For each fault type, we construct a reduced-order, two-dimensional model in terms of the apparent voltage and current seen by the relay. This makes all computations efficient, aligns with standard quantities in distance protection, and allows us to define instantaneous fault tests in terms of unsolved differential equations.

</details>

Abstract: The integration of inverter-based renewable energy sources into electric grids challenges conventional power system protection. Machine learning-based solutions can address these challenges by utilizing available data in modern smart grids. However, the lack of open datasets prevents reproducibility and fair comparisons between different approaches and their results, which hinders further progress. Therefore, this paper presents EvEMTBench, a synthetic dataset of faults and events generated using electromagnetic transient simulations. The physical plausibility of the power system simulation is ensured by validating the simulation parameters against established literature and providing comprehensive documentation of the simulation procedure. The dataset is designed for training, fine-tuning, and benchmarking machine learning models by providing synchronized point-on-wave voltage and current measurements at 9600 Hz across a diverse set of topologies and voltage levels. The inclusion of a wide range of fault and operating events allows the utilization of EvEMTBench for different tasks like incipient fault detection, fault localization, or event detection.

Conclusion: 在完全可审计的评估中，这些结果证明了无需人类介入即可实现运行时异常情况管理，为工业过程安全自主性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Automation operates safety-critical processes inside their design envelope and leaves abnormal situations to human operators. Mismanagement of these situations is a leading contributor to process-safety incidents and a hindrance to achieving autonomy. Here we show that a general-purpose large reasoning model, with no task-specific training and only the information available to an operator, manages abnormal situations at run time through a bounded, programmatically verified action interface. Across 39 abnormal situations and operating-point changes on a plant-wide industrial benchmark process, the reasoning model maintained the plant within all hard constraints in all 39, while basic regulatory control failed in 15. It matched the plant's expert-engineered advanced control and diagnosed the root-cause fault in 15 of 15 safety-critical situations. Three independently developed models spanning a thirty-fold cost range exceeded the baseline. In a fully auditable evaluation, these results demonstrate run-time abnormal situation management without a human in the loop.

</details>

Method: 基于固定结构高斯混合滤波框架，通过离线将转移密度分解为轴向对齐高斯分量来确定预测密度结构；对受异常值影响的测量分量采用Student's-t分布建模，并使用变分贝叶斯过程近似更新每个高斯混合分量。

Result: 在三维跟踪场景中（具有距离和方位角测量），使用重尾噪声模拟异常值，验证了所提出滤波器在异常值存在情况下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该滤波器通过将高斯混合结构保持为确定性且可通过分解保真度调节，结合Student's-t分布和变分贝叶斯更新，实现了对测量异常值的鲁棒状态估计。

Abstract: Bayesian state estimation for discrete-time nonlinear stochastic systems is considered in the presence of measurement outliers. Building on a fixed-structure Gaussian mixture filtering framework, this paper proposes a robust measurement-update variant in which the predictive density structure is determined by an offline decomposition of the transition density into axis-aligned Gaussian components. This construction maintains the Gaussian mixture structure as deterministic and tunable via the chosen decomposition fidelity. Measurement components affected by outliers are modeled using a Student's-t distribution, and the corresponding update of each Gaussian mixture component is approximated by a variational Bayes procedure. The resulting filter is evaluated in a three-dimensional tracking scenario with range and bearing measurements, where the bearing channel is affected by outliers, modeled as heavy-tailed noise.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谐波相互作用已成为换流器主导电力系统中一个关键的动态稳定性现象，但目前缺乏在非线性动力系统背景下对谐波稳定性的正式定义，这阻碍了系统性的稳定性分析与评估。

Method: 1) 提出谐波稳定性的严格定义，将其重构为BIBO稳定性与内部稳定性的组合；2) 严格证明非线性CBPS任意名义周期轨迹的局部谐波稳定性可由其线性时周期（LTP）近似的稳定性性质所蕴含；3) 基于谐波状态空间（HSS）表示LTP模型，建立计算可行的稳定性评估框架，提供时不变的线性矩阵不等式（LMI）条件来证明谐波稳定性。

Result: 在电网跟随型换流器系统上进行了仿真验证，HSS分析数值上证实了周期运行轨迹周围的谐波稳定性，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Harmonic interactions have become a defining dynamic stability phenomenon in converter-based power systems (CBPSs). However, a formalization of the notion of harmonic stability in the context of nonlinear dynamical systems is not available thus far. In this paper, we propose a definition of harmonic stability formulated as a combination of two standard stability notions, namely, bounded-input bounded-output (BIBO) and internal stability properties with respect to any nominal periodic trajectory. Moreover, we rigorously show that the local harmonic stability of any nominal periodic trajectory of nonlinear CBPSs is implied by the stability properties of its linear time-periodic (LTP) approximation. In addition, to enable computationally tractable stability assessment, we develop a framework based on harmonic state-space (HSS) representations of LTP models. In particular, we provide time-invariant linear matrix inequality (LMI) conditions to certify harmonic stability. The proposed methodology is illustrated on a grid-following converter system, where the HSS-based analysis numerically confirms harmonic stability around the periodic operating trajectory.

</details>


### [73] [Validation of a driver model for energy consumption simulations of road vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19980)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的车辆能量性能评估依赖驾驶循环（预设速度剖面），但近期研究提出基于运行条件的替代框架。该框架需要将运行条件转化为速度剖面，而这一转化过程依赖于驾驶员建模，这正是现有研究中的空白。

Method: 提出一个简单的驾驶员模型，使其兼容运行条件框架；通过受控驾驶模拟器研究和真实世界车辆日志数据进行模型验证，利用真实数据识别模型参数并评估预测性能。

Result: 结果表明该简单模型能够有效重现驾驶员行为的主要影响，但需要进一步研究来全面评估其有效性。

Conclusion: 简单驾驶员模型在运行条件框架下具有可行性，能捕捉驾驶员行为的关键特征，但仍需更多研究验证其全面有效性。

Abstract: The energy performance of road vehicles has traditionally been evaluated using driving cycles, in which a prescribed speed profile is tracked either in simulation or by a physical vehicle. Recent research has proposed an alternative framework based on operating conditions, where the driving environment is described in terms of factors such as road topography, legal speed limits, traffic, and weather, rather than by an explicit speed profile. This paper addresses the complementary problem of driver modeling, necessary to translate the operating conditions to a speed profile. A simple driver model designed to be compatible with the operating-condition framework and evaluates its ability to reproduce realistic driving behavior is investigated. Validation of the model is done through a controlled driving-simulator study vehicle log files collected during real-world operation and are used to identify the model parameters and assess the model's predictive performance. The results suggest that this simple model can be efficient in reproducing major effects of driver behavior, while further research is required to fully assess its validity.

</details>


### [74] [Wave-Based Bilateral Teleoperation between Nonlinear Manipulators with Direct Contact Force Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20043)
*G. Q. Bao Tran,Takanori Miyoshi,Ho Duc Tho*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性多自由度机械臂在恒定通信时延下的双边遥操作系统。与经典波变换架构不同，本文将环境力反射到主端以增强透明度，通过LMI方法表征Euler-Lagrange远程系统的无源性不足，并采用上严格无源通信律补偿该不足，从而在时延下保持闭环稳定性以及位置和力同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典波变换架构传输协调力而非直接力反馈，牺牲了遥操作的透明度。直接接触力反馈虽能提升透明度，但可能破坏闭环稳定性。因此需要在保持稳定性的同时实现力反射，这是一个核心权衡问题。

Method: 1) 利用LMI方法对Euler-Lagrange远程系统建立无源性不足（passivity shortage）的表征；2) 设计上严格无源（upper strictly passive）通信律来补偿计算出的无源性不足；3) 在适当条件下保证时延下的闭环稳定性以及位置和力同步。

Result: 通过仿真验证了所提方法在非线性2自由度机械臂不同配置下的有效性，表明在存在通信时延的情况下，系统能够保持稳定性并实现位置与力的同步。

Conclusion: 该研究为在时延环境下实现高透明度遥操作提供了新的理论框架，通过量化无源性不足并针对性补偿，突破了直接力反馈与稳定性之间的传统冲突。

Abstract: We study bilateral teleoperation between nonlinear, multi-DOF robotic manipulators in the presence of constant communication delays. Unlike classical wave-transformation architectures that transmit a coordinating force, we consider the case where the environmental force is reflected to the master side to enhance teleoperation transparency. Since direct contact force feedback might destabilize the closed-loop system, we first develop a passivity-shortage characterization for the Euler--Lagrange remote system using a linear matrix inequality (LMI) approach. An upper strictly passive communication law is then employed to compensate for the computed passivity shortage so that the closed-loop stability under delays as well as position and force synchronization are preserved under appropriate conditions. Simulations with nonlinear 2-DOF robotic manipulators in different settings illustrate our approach.

</details>
